# Coverage

One comparison for each skill. The neutral id names the source role. It is not a folder name and it is not a product name.

Words are whitespace-separated tokens in `SKILL.md` after the frontmatter, plus every text file under `references/`. An 8-gram is eight consecutive tokens made of letters, digits, and apostrophes, compared in lowercase. The overlap column is that count between our skill text and the paired source text. It is 0 for every skill.

A row is one source rule. The tool cell is the Dreamstate capability or definition id this desk uses for that rule, `local` when the work stays in a file or script on this desk, or `none` when the rule is a writing limit. Mapped means this desk states the rule. A gap means this desk does not perform it. When the rule is kept and the old call has no Dreamstate replacement, the tool cell still says `gap: Dreamstate can't do this`.

`column.ai` is still to-check. Do not call it.

| R11 | A successful create does not prove the host finished processing or that the picture is public | body: a successful create is not proof processing finished; do not invent a public address | none |

Channel list, not a source rule in this pair: `social.channels_list` also includes Threads, TikTok, Instagram, Facebook, and Reddit. Reddit schedule and publish are draft-only. This skill does not schedule or publish Reddit. No source rule in `src-video-publish` named those channels.

|---|---|---|---|

| R03 | Take out aborted openings, long quiet, and on-screen hunting or setup | Defaults: starts they abandoned, long dead air, and setup or search transitions on screen | none |

| R10 | Set min_seconds to 9999 so pause trimming stays off; cutting quiet while someone types or clicks makes the picture jump | Boundaries: min_seconds to 9999 | local |
| R11 | Inventory each named file with ffprobe; follow a stated order, otherwise timestamp order | Render: ffprobe for width, height, and avg_frame_rate; Retakes: chronological order is the default | local |
| R12 | Transcribe only the named files at word level; if the audio is not allowed to leave, cut from acoustic pauses alone | Render: any word-timed transcript is enough; cut from acoustic pauses alone | none |
| R13 | Anchor each window on a word start, with 60 ms of lead-in and 150 ms of tail | Boundaries: 60 ms before the first word and 150 ms after the last word | none |
| R14 | Also mark what the detector misses: a hunt on screen, a spoken bridge, a restart that failed, and a setup line said again | Manual drops: gaps silence cannot see | none |

| G01 | Their client and its scripts, including profile, org, toolkit names, search and lookup action ids, per-call credit caps, and retries | gap: that client is not this desk | gap: Dreamstate can't do this |
| G02 | Author-scoped search batches and the 480 or 512 character search cap | gap: that search syntax is not used | gap: the stored-post list takes handles, at most 100 per call |
| G03 | Paid keyword pages and focus-query pages, including how many results per page and the budget talk tied to per-post billing | gap: that billing model is not used | gap: off-list floors, lane fit, and the focus-lane gate stay for pastes and stored posts |
| G04 | The three example reply pairs | gap: example replies were not copied | gap: sample sentences are not stored |
| G05 | Publishing through their page host to a stored page URL | gap: that host is not this desk | gap: Dreamstate can't do this |
| G06 | Downloading images from the image host, and the per-image and per-page byte caps on that download | gap: that download is not done | gap: the page shows bytes already in the payload |
| G07 | Filling the move list with their verbatim sentences | gap: an edit becomes a voice rule, not a stored copy of their sentence | gap: those sentences are not saved in the move list |
| G08 | Asking a question in place of a missing receipt | gap: this desk skips when there is no receipt | gap: a question-only draft is not the substitute |
| G09 | Warning on the card at 280 characters | gap: the lint gate is 200 | gap: the page warns over 200 |
| G10 | A block on the page for replies under the owner's own posts | gap: those replies are not queued | gap: they answer them in the first hour |
| G11 | Pointers to the source kit's sibling skill names | gap: those names are not used | gap: this desk points at x-draft, x-week, x-scan, and comment-queue |

## post-path

Neutral id: `src-post-path`. Source words: 448. Our words: 699. 8-gram overlap: 0.

| # | Source rule | Our rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|---|
| R01 | Use real person-profile links, preview the setup and the per-cycle ceiling, and keep the hash that comes back | (body) — Preview is free, max 10 real person URLs, returns preview_hash, no network | tables.profile_watcher.preview |
| R02 | Creating or resuming that watcher is paid recurring setup and needs an explicit yes | (body) — Create and resume need that hash, approved true, and yes, because later runs spend. Do not create one in this skill | tables.profile_watcher.create |
| R03 | Do not stand up a second multi-table collector for the same profiles | No second collector for the same profiles | none |
| R04 | One stable request id across an ambiguous retry, then read the run back, and do not open a second watcher | (body) — Timeout: runs_get on the same idempotency_key. No second watcher | runs_get |
| R05 | Score only after the real columns and both rubrics are in front of you | (body) — Score only with both rubrics present. Bind only keys the sheet has | none |
| R06 | The score reads evidence already on the row. It is a separate paid run. It does not invent facts | (body) — The score reads those cells. Dry-run tables.columns.run. A required unknown is a hold | tables.columns.run |
| R07 | Calibrate a bounded sample before a standing auto-run | (body) — Include a fit, a company fail, a person fail, and an empty required field. tables.auto_run.set is separate | tables.columns.run |
| R08 | Hold when required enrichment is missing | (body) — A required unknown is a hold, not a no and not a yes. An empty required field stays a hold | none |
| R09 | Company fit and person fit stay independent | (body) — Add two column.ai_fit_score columns, company and person, each 0 to 100. A company fail stops the row even when the person passes. Do not call column.ai | tables.columns.add |
| R10 | Keep that person's source post on the same row | (body) — One row holds the profile and the post | visibility.linkedin.engagement_harvest |
| R11 | Collection, qualification, resource delivery, and enrollment do not perform each other | (body) — It messages nobody. A pass is not a yes to send. Ask goes to magnet-send. Do not call sequence.sequences.activate_audience | none |
| R12 | A later lookup uses only the named research calls. Do not swap in another provider | (body) — A later lookup is visibility.research.web_search (query) or visibility.research.urls_fetch (urls) | visibility.research.web_search |
| R13 | Keep the post id and read delivery state back when the post is a stored artifact | (body) — Live post: keep post id. Stored artifact: social.content.artifact_get | social.content.artifact_get |
| R14 | Name the capture window, and do not walk older posts to fill a blank | (body) — Published posts, in a window you name. Do not walk older posts for a blank column | visibility.linkedin.engagement_harvest |
| R15 | Capture is not auto-enrollment | (body) — Hold is not enrolled and is not sent. Do not call sequence.sequences.activate_audience | none |
| R16 | A standing outreach yes names the cohort, the destination sequence, the limits, and the stops | (body) — Do not call sequence.sequences.activate_audience. A warm note after both scores pass follows warm-cadence: capped, no pitch, owner yes on that text, one question, no offer or calendar link unless they asked | sequence.sequences.activate_audience |
| R17 | Engagement alone is not fit and is not permission to widen a send | (body) — Engagement is not fit. A reaction is not intent to buy | none |
| R18 | Do not trust an empty owned-post comment queue until freshness is checked | (body) — Comments on the owner's post: social.comments.queue_list. Trust an empty queue only after visibility.linkedin.comments.refresh (delivery_id). Articles and newsletters return 422 | visibility.linkedin.comments.refresh |
| R19 | Reply through a preview and a content-bound approval. Do not mint an approval. If the effect is unknown, read the run and do not retry blind | (body) — Preview with social.comments.reply. social.comments.approve after yes on that text: action_id, a 64-hex content_hash, and approved true. If the effect is unknown, read the run and do not resend | social.comments.approve |

## inbox-desk

Neutral id: `src-inbox`. Source words: 225. Our words: 415. 8-gram overlap: 0.

| # | Source rule | Our rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|---|
| R01 | Sort threads already in progress, ground every note, and send only for the account and thread the owner named. | opening: Triage threads that are already open. Stay on the account and the thread the owner named. Ground every class, and any note you pass on. Do not send. A send needs the owner's yes on that text. | none |
| R02 | Read the operating contract before acting. | opening: Follow docs/skill-frame.md. Ids are in docs/tool-map.md. tools_get before tools_run. The fetched schema wins. | tools_get |
| R03 | This desk owns email, LinkedIn, and WhatsApp threads that already exist, plus one email that is not on a cadence. | opening: The set is email, LinkedIn, WhatsApp, and one email that is not on a cadence. | none |
| R04 | A first LinkedIn conversation, and any outreach program, belongs on the cadence skill. | opening: A new LinkedIn conversation, and any outreach program, goes to warm-cadence. | none |
| R05 | Request a small page. Cut by channel, time, status, one person, or a shared tag when the schema still has those fields. | opening: List with unibox.inbox.unified_list. Keep each page small. When those fields are still on the schema, narrow by channel, time, status, one participant, or a shared tag. | unibox.inbox.unified_list |
| R06 | With no status cut, answered threads come back too. A reply queue uses the unanswered tab. | opening: With no status cut, the list also returns threads that already have an answer. For a reply queue, pass tab needs_action when that enum is still in the schema. | unibox.inbox.unified_list |
| R07 | Read the whole thread that matters, and the company pages that bear on it, before a class or a note. | opening: Read the whole thread that matters, and the Company Context pages that bear on it, before a classification or a draft. | none |
| R08 | Keep the thread id, the people, the sending account, and the delivery state the call returned. | opening: Keep the provider thread id, the participants, the sending account, and the delivery state the call returned. | none |
| R09 | Review one thread on the real review call. It costs 1 credit and does not send. | opening: For a review of one thread, unibox.inbox.analyze costs 1 credit and never sends. Required conversation_id. Optional analysis_mode (classify_only or draft_reply, default draft_reply), account_id, message_id. dry_run, then live with quote_hash, max_credits, and an idempotency_key. Spend that credit on one conversation. | unibox.inbox.analyze |
| R10 | LinkedIn rows are limited to the sender you resolved. | opening: LinkedIn rows are visible for the sender you resolved. | none |
| R11 | If expected threads are missing, inspect that account again through the account lists. | opening: If expected threads are missing, look again with senders.linkedin_accounts.list and senders.social_accounts.list. Do not call senders.identities.inbox_scope_get. | senders.linkedin_accounts.list |
| R12 | An empty allowlist is not the whole inbox. Do not widen ingestion just to find more threads. | opening: It is an email mailbox allowlist. An empty result is not an empty LinkedIn inbox. Do not read that empty allowlist as the whole inbox. Do not widen what that account ingests just to locate extra threads. | none |
| R13 | A reply carries the exact text, the thread, the account, and the recipients. Pass one thread on. | opening: A reply carries the exact text, the thread, the account, and the recipients. Hand one thread to thread-reply. | none |
| R14 | Send only after the owner allows that text. | opening: Do not send. A send needs the owner's yes on that text, on the account and the thread the owner named. | none |
| R15 | A label edit, an archive, or a bulk change shows the real count and the scope first, and leaves every other thread alone. Do not invent an id for it. | opening: A label edit, an archive, or a bulk change shows the real count and the scope first, and leaves every conversation outside that scope untouched. Do not invent a capability id for it. | none |
| R16 | A timeout does not prove the note never arrived. | opening: A timeout does not prove the message was never delivered. | none |
| R17 | Before a retry, read the returned message and the run record. | opening: Before a retry, read the returned message and the run with runs_get. | runs_get |
| R18 | A public comment on a post is not this inbox. | opening: A comment posted in public on a post goes to comment-queue. | none |
| R19 | Report only the slice you opened: each class, a draft only if you handed one thread on, a link only if a tool returned one, and threads still open. | opening: Report only the slice you opened: each classification, the draft when you handed one thread to thread-reply, a message link only when the tool returned one, and the threads still unresolved. | none |
| R20 | On an error, do not file feedback and do not copy the thread or its transcript anywhere else. | opening: On an error, do not file feedback and do not copy the conversation or its transcript anywhere else. | none |
| G01 | Search a catalog phrase for the triage command, then load the chosen inbox command. | gap: that client search is not how this desk discovers a call | gap: tools_get before tools_run |

## thread-reply

Neutral id: `src-thread-reply`. Source words: 1530. Our words: 1697. 8-gram overlap: 0.

| # | Source rule | Our rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|---|
| R01 | One reply, the thread link beside it, and nothing goes out until the owner says yes to that exact text. | opening: One reply, one open thread, thread link beside the draft. Nothing goes out until the owner accepts that exact text. | unibox.context.thread_reply_send |
| R02 | Read the workspace notes, brand, and audience. Voice lives with the brand file and the vault. The failing shapes live in this skill. | opening: Read AGENTS.md, then filled files brand/AGENTS.md points at, then audience/AGENTS.md and audience.local/ideal-follower.md when fit matters. For a DM, compress brand.local/voice.md and vault/voice/ samples. No reply shapes in a side file. These rules are the set. | none |
| R03 | An existing thread uses the inbox tools. A new conversation or a cadence is the sequence skill. | opening: Existing threads (docs/conversations.md). A new conversation or cadence is warm-cadence. Listing the slice is inbox-desk. | none |
| R04 | Compress the local voice and reuse this thread's earlier owner lines. The shapes that fail are in this skill. | How it should sound: compress brand.local/voice.md and vault/voice/ samples, and reuse this thread's earlier owner lines. These shapes fail, composed from this thread. | none |
| R05 | A direct message is usually under 240 characters and one to four short paragraphs. A comment on someone else's post is one to three lines. A long post goes to the post skill. | opening: A direct message is usually under 240 characters, one to four short paragraphs. A comment on someone else's post is comment-queue: one to three lines, almost never a pitch. A long post or an article is linkedin-draft. An own-post reply is social.comments.reply, then after yes social.comments.approve with action_id, a 64-hex content_hash, and approved true. | social.comments.approve |
| R06 | Answer whatever they asked last, including a limit or the stack they use. If they are still deciding, stay on that point and ask which part of the setup is next. Product detail waits until they request it. | How it should sound: Answer the last message, including any constraint or stack they named. While a buyer stays in the thread, answer what they just said and ask which setup part comes next. Describe the product only after they ask. | none |
| R07 | Everyday words, precise nouns, the owner's informal rhythm. No slang quota. Each link on its own line. No sign-off. | How it should sound: Everyday words, precise nouns, the owner's informal rhythm. No slang quota. Each link on its own line. No sign-off. | none |
| R08 | When they asked for the repo, the recording, or the link, put it in that same message. | How it should sound: A requested repo, recording, or link goes in that same message. Hand the file over while fit is still open. Fit is not a toll on the file. | none |
| R09 | Sound like a peer. Credit work they already did. Compare tools fairly. | How it should sound: Sound like a peer. Name work already done. Compare tools without a slant. | none |
| R10 | Admit a gap when it is real. Do not invent a demo, a date, a capability, a price, a discount, or a plan. Hold the one missing fact. | How it should sound: If unsure, say so. Never invent a demo, date, capability, price, discount, or plan the owner has not stated. One missing fact: stop, ask, leave the gap. | none |
| R11 | No calendar push by default. A call link only if they asked, or the call itself is only a question for someone who already qualifies. | How it should sound: No calendar link unless they asked. A qualified prospect gets the answer, then a call as a question. Still no calendar link unless they asked. A meeting is integrations.calendar.events_create. It emails attendees. Required connection_id, title, start_at, end_at. 0 credits. No dry_run. Owner yes first. | integrations.calendar.events_create |
| R12 | One next step only. The closing question comes from what they said, not a stock line. | How it should sound: One next step only: the link, a question, or what they asked you to hand them. That question comes from their words, not a reused line. One close. Do not stack a second ask. | none |
| R13 | Match their language and register. | How it should sound: Their language and register. | none |
| R14 | Do not paste a tool's suggested reply, including a draft from the review call. Rewrite from the thread. | How it should sound: Do not paste a tool reply, including draft_reply from unibox.inbox.analyze. Compose again from this thread, in the owner's words. Analyze costs 1 credit and never sends. | unibox.inbox.analyze |
| R15 | When their last note is more than three days old, mention the gap briefly, and give the true cause if you have one. Do not open every draft the same way. | How it should sound: Older than three days: name the gap in a short clause, and the true cause if you know it. Change wording on every draft. One shared apology sounds automatic. | none |
| R16 | No em dash. A period, a comma, or and. | How it should sound: No em dash. Use a period, a comma, or and. | none |
| R17 | Status filters are hints, not proof. Skip a thread whose newest line is outbound or whose order is unclear. Read history for links, questions, signups, and meetings. An outbound line may be automation. Read another stored thread for that person first. | How it should sound: A status chip or due filter is only a hint. Reread the thread. Skip when the newest line went out from this account, or line order is unclear. Catch a link already sent, a question, a signup, or a meeting already promised. Before an outbound line counts as the owner's voice, confirm a sequence or other automation did not write it. Read any other stored thread for that person first. | none |
| R18 | A resource request gets the link plus one question about their work. The same file in five or more threads goes to the delivery skill. | How it should sound: A resource request gets the link plus one question about the work in front of them. The same file in five or more threads: stop the one-off copies and hand delivery to magnet-send. | none |
| R19 | A compliment or a new connection gets a short thanks and a question about what they are working on. | How it should sound: A compliment or a new connection gets thanks plus what they are working on. | none |
| R20 | A qualified prospect gets the answer, then the call as a question. They fit the buyer page and named a concrete need. | How it should sound: A qualified prospect gets the answer, then a call as a question. They fit the buyer page and named a need you can point at. | none |
| R21 | A weaker fit, or a fit you cannot score, gets the answer and a self-serve path, with help if a later question shows up. | How it should sound: A weaker fit, and a fit you still cannot score, gets the answer plus self-serve (the site, or the signup). Offer help if a later question shows up. | none |
| R22 | A customer gets help where they are stuck. This desk does not push a meeting unless they asked. | How it should sound: A customer, including someone already signed up, gets help where they are stuck. No meeting push unless they asked. | none |
| R23 | A thanks, a done after they booked, or a note that they will look later gets no reply. | How it should sound: Nothing to answer means no reply: a thanks, a done after they booked, or a note they will look later. | none |
| R24 | Support thanks them and asks which step failed. Do not ship a fix. A refund or an account change waits with the owner. | How it should sound: Support acknowledges and asks which step failed. It does not ship a fix. A refund or an account change waits with the owner. | none |
| R25 | Someone pitching the owner gets no reply unless the owner asked. | How it should sound: A pitch means no reply unless they asked. | none |
| R26 | An event, a show, a partnership, a payment, the law, or a private matter waits for the owner. | How it should sound: An event, a show, a partnership, a payment, the law, or a private matter goes to the owner. Do not promise a fee, a date, or a yes. | none |
| R27 | Qualify on company, role, a stated need, and intent. A famous company or a senior title alone fails. Unknown is a hold. Someone ready to buy moves to the next product step. | How it should sound: Need a fitting company, a role you can name, a need they stated, and a sign they mean to act. A famous company, or a top title with nothing else, leaves them unqualified. Unknown stays a hold, not a pass. Ready to buy: move them to the following product step. Do not park a meeting in front of it. | none |
| R28 | List a bounded slice of interested threads that are still unanswered, and say how much of the inbox that slice covered. | How it should sound: Open a bounded set of interested threads still unanswered. inbox-desk reads unibox.inbox.unified_list, tab needs_action, when that value is still in the schema. State how much of the inbox the slice covered. Do not audit the whole mailbox. | unibox.inbox.unified_list |
| R29 | Read and route each thread before drafting. | How it should sound: Route each thread, then three blocks. | none |
| R30 | Lead with rows that are ready: who, the link, their latest point, why they fit, the route, and the draft inside a fence so line breaks stay. Then rows you held and the fact still needed. Then how many were already answered, and how many were noise. Buyers before the rest. Inside one route, the older thread first. | How it should sound: Ready: name, channel, account, recipient, thread link, point of the latest line, fit evidence, route, and the draft in a fenced block so breaks remain. Held: the missing fact. Then counts of answered threads and of noise. Buyers first. Inside one route, older thread first. unibox.inbox.reply_draft_edit saves the draft and does not send. | unibox.inbox.reply_draft_edit |
| R31 | Reread the thread before you show the draft and again before a send. A newer line retires the draft. A wording change after yes needs a new yes. | How it should sound: Reread once before the draft and once before a send. Any newer inbound or outbound line retires the draft. Send only the approved text. Wording changed after yes: ask again on the new text. expected_revision comes from unibox.inbox.reply_drafts_list. Free read, no required args. No dry_run. | unibox.inbox.reply_drafts_list |
| R32 | Space accepted sends by several minutes, and stay inside that account's limits. When the outcome is unclear, open the thread again before trying again. | How it should sound: A few minutes between accepted sends, inside the account's own limits. One live send per call. unibox.context.thread_reply_send needs owner yes on that text. client_request_id is a new UUID. max_live_sends is 1. Unclear result: open the thread again before any retry. | unibox.context.thread_reply_send |
| R33 | Report what was sent, skipped, failed, and held. Count a sent link apart from a signup, a first product action, and a booking that was actually qualified. | How it should sound: At the end, report what went out, what you skipped, what failed, and what you held. Tally a sent link apart from a signup, a first product action, and a booking that was actually qualified. | none |
| R34 | If the owner changes a draft or turns it down, save the general lesson without names and without the message, and store the wording they kept as a sample. | How it should sound: If the owner rewrites or rejects a draft, record the general rule in brand.local/voice.md, without names or message text. Store kept wording under vault/voice/. | none |
| R35 | A repeating pass may draft the queue. It does not send. Keep its state outside the repo. One loop only. Advance the marker only after every fetched thread is handled or held. A streak or elapsed time does not turn sending on. | How it should sound: A repeating pass may build the queue on a short interval the owner chose. It drafts and shows. It does not send. Outside the repo, store last run, open drafts, each judgment, and a private do-not-answer list. One loop only. Advance the marker only after each fetched thread is handled or held. Approval stays until they name cases that may send. A streak or elapsed time does not turn sending on. | none |
| R36 | The morning list is name, one line they last wrote, the route, and the link. Ask which to draft unless they already said to begin. | How it should sound: Morning list: each interested or still-unanswered thread as a name, one line they last wrote, the route, and the link. Ask which to draft. Draft only after they said to begin. | none |
| R37 | A paid profile or company read used only to personalize is quoted first and run only after yes. | How it should sound: A profile or company read only to personalize is paid. Quote first. An incomplete duplicate check is not a send. | none |
| R28 | Only a one-to-one chat counts, at most two pages of twenty. Proof is a message you sent, still present, whose text contains the destination. Read about the last twenty messages. Accepted but not seen on read-back stays unverified. No chat id on the receipt means review, not a second send. | opening: Only a one-to-one chat counts. At most two chat pages, twenty at a time. Proof is a message you sent, still present, containing the destination. Read about the last twenty messages. Accepted but not yet on read-back stays unverified. No chat id on the receipt means review, not a second send. | none |
| R29 | Order: file message, read it back, then the public line. Save intent on the worksheet before every external write. Add the receipt column after fetching the schema. Without that column the first real send has nothing to reconcile against. Empty previews can still pass. | opening: File message first. Read it back. Then the public line. Save intent on the worksheet before every external write. Add the receipt column with tables.columns.add after tools_get. Without that column, the first real comment has nothing to reconcile against. Empty previews can still pass. | tables.columns.add |
| R30 | An uncertain send is reviewed and is not repeated. Public lines wait until the file messages of that pass are done, so a timeout only cuts the tail. | opening: An uncertain send is reviewed, not repeated. Finish private messages before any public line, so a timeout only cuts the tail. | none |
| R31 | A do-not-contact flag blocks only a new file message. If no suppression tool is fetched, search, then fetch the schema. Do not invent an id. A cut-short page, or a page that fills its cap, refuses the send. Match provider id or profile slug, ignoring a trailing slash and a query. A flag that only records an inbound reply does not block the file they asked for. After the file is verified, their reply stops later automation. The public line still goes out. | opening: A do-not-contact flag blocks only a new file message. Suppression tool missing: search, then tools_get. Do not invent an id. A cut-short list, or a full page, refuses the send. About five hundred rows on a page is incomplete. Match provider id or profile slug, ignoring a trailing slash and a query. A flag that only records an inbound reply does not block the file they asked for. After the file is verified, their reply stops later automation. The public line still goes out. | tools_get |
| R32 | Message shape: a real first name, a blank line, a line that names the file, a blank line, the full destination URL. The noun is the file's name. No guessed name. | opening: Message shape, with a real first name: that name, a blank line, a line naming this file, a blank line, the full destination URL. The noun is the file's name. No real first token: a neutral stand-in. Do not guess a name. | none |
| R33 | Leave pacing at the account's own spacing. Do not tighten it. Do not invent a spacing call. Read the account list. Use limits, today's usage, and working hours only when that payload includes them. | opening: Pacing stays at the account's own spacing. Do not tighten it. Do not invent a spacing API. Read senders.linkedin_accounts.list. Use limits, today's usage, and working hours only when present; if absent, say so. | senders.linkedin_accounts.list |
| R34 | A shorter gap is refused and burns the cycle. About twenty to thirty actions an hour, about ten people. The owner may shorten the gap. This skill never does. | opening: A shorter gap is refused and burns the cycle. Expect twenty to thirty actions an hour, about ten people. The owner may shorten the gap for safety. This skill never does. | none |
| R35 | Stay inside working hours and stop a few minutes before they end. If the account names working days, skip the others. If the zone does not match, the hours check did not run. Saturday and Sunday are half volume unless the account marks the weekend full. A health factor between zero and one lowers the cap, floor one. A warmup cap counts. Leave two message slots in reserve. | opening: Stay inside working hours and stop a few minutes early. Skip days the account does not name as working. If your zone is not the account's zone, the hours check did not run. Say so. Saturday and Sunday are half unless the weekend is full volume. A health factor from zero to one lowers the cap the same way. Floor is one, not zero. Count the warmup cap. Leave two message slots in reserve. | none |
| R36 | When the action cap is spent, stop file messages and public lines. The message cap and the warmup cap stop file messages. The comment cap stops public lines. If the account state is unknown and this pass already wrote once, wait about two minutes. If it names a next slot, wait it out, plus a few seconds. A spacing, rate-limit, or hours refusal that is not an unknown outcome puts the row back to waiting. | opening: Action cap spent: stop file messages and public lines. Message or warmup cap: stop file messages. Comment cap: stop public lines. Unknown account state after one write: wait about two minutes. A named next slot: wait it out, plus a few seconds. A spacing, rate-limit, or hours refusal that is not unknown returns the row to waiting. | none |
| R37 | One pass, unless filling missing public lines: about 150 comments inspected, 12 file messages, 6 connect-first lines, 12 sent-lines. More than 500 comments stops the scan. Comment reads can lock for hours, and then nothing goes out. A busy post stretches the pass from about fifteen minutes toward thirty. Check the worksheet and the send list, not a hand re-read. Page the queue. Do not scrape in a tight loop. The comment list needs account id and post id. A dry run executes live. | opening: One pass, unless only filling missing public lines: about 150 comments, 12 file messages, 6 connect-first lines, 12 sent-lines. More than 500 comments stops the scan on purpose. Once busy, stretch toward thirty minutes, not fifteen. Progress: the worksheet and social.outreach.keyword_dm_sends.list. Do not re-read comments while delivery is live. social.comments.queue_list is paged. Use its cursor. Do not scrape in a tight loop. social.linkedin_posts.comments_list needs account_id and post_id. dry_run runs live. | social.linkedin_posts.comments_list |
| R38 | If the schema offers a recent sort and a relevant sort, read both and merge on comment id. The recent sort can omit the newest comments. If the relevant read fails, keep the recent list. When the post lists more people than the worksheet, compare the two sorts first. | opening: If recent and relevant sorts both exist, read both and merge on comment id. Newest can be missing from recent. If relevant fails, keep recent. If the post lists more people than the worksheet, compare sorts before calling it a miss. | social.linkedin_posts.comments_list |
| R39 | Do not share a quarter-hour with another job on the same account. The one that loses looks like an uncertain write, and nothing went out. You do not set that clock. | opening: No other job on this account in the same quarter-hour. They fight for the account gap; the loser looks like an uncertain write, and nothing went out. You do not set that clock. | none |
| R40 | On a crowded post: uncertain file messages, then first-degree people who still lack the file, including people who connected after the connect-first line, then public lines. Same priority: the person checked least recently. Public lines last, or replies fill the cap and the file sits for days. | opening: Crowded post: uncertain file messages, then first-degree people still missing the file, including people who connected after the connect-first line, then public lines. Tie: the person checked least recently. Public lines last, or replies spend the cap and the file sits for days. | none |
| R41 | Reply-thread reads go last, at most three per pass. A pass that dies inside one pauses those reads for three hours. After a rate limit, pause them for hours. The thread endpoint can be limited on its own while the comment list still answers. | opening: Reply-thread reads at the end, cap three. A pass that dies inside one pauses those reads for three hours. After a rate limit, pause them for hours. The thread endpoint can limit while the comment list still answers. | none |
| R42 | One pass at a time. A pass more than nine minutes after its slot is stale. Skip it. An unconfirmed write may be tried once more after fifteen minutes, then it waits for review. Two tries is the ceiling. At about a thousand worksheet rows, stop and review before widening the list. | opening: One pass at a time. More than nine minutes after its slot: stale, skip. An unconfirmed write may be tried once more after fifteen minutes, then review. Two tries is the ceiling. About a thousand worksheet rows: stop and review before widening. | local |
| R43 | Resolve a short post link to the post id, then read the post. Cost 0. No dry run. Use the id that read returns. | opening: A short post link: resolve the post id, then social.linkedin_posts.get (account_id, post_id). Cost 0. It calls the network and has no dry_run. Do not send that mode. Use the returned id. | social.linkedin_posts.get |
| R44 | One comment: preview the public reply and draft the private message, and get yes on both texts. Approving posts the public reply. | opening: One comment: social.comments.reply plus a drafted private message, yes on both texts. social.comments.approve posts the public reply (action_id, content_hash, approved true). | social.comments.approve |
| R45 | A preview pulls the live comment list and stores nothing. Its plan names each file message and each public line. Read it before any pass that can send. Anything that is not a live pass must not send. A stubbed dry run that shows zero comments only proves the loader. Do not set a credit ceiling that finishes the file message and then drops the public line, unless public lines are paused on purpose. | opening: A preview pulls the live comment list and stores no row. It plans each file message and public line. Read that plan before any pass that can send. Anything that is not a live pass must not send. A dry run that stubs the network and shows zero comments only proves the loader ran. Do not set a credit ceiling that finishes the file message and drops the public line, unless those lines are paused on purpose. | none |
| R46 | People already finished stay marked done. An acknowledgement-only pass posts missing public lines for verified deliveries, sends no new file message and no new connect-first line, inspects about a hundred, and may post up to about forty sent-lines. A request backfill names at most a hundred people and cannot run in the same pass. Public lines may stay off while file messages continue. Turning them back on needs a yes, then one pass for the lines that never posted. | opening: Finished people stay done. An acknowledgement-only pass posts missing public lines for people whose file is verified: no new file message, no new connect-first line, about a hundred comments, up to about forty sent-lines. A request backfill names people, at most a hundred, and cannot share a pass with acknowledgement-only. Public lines may stay off while comment writes time out. File messages continue. Turn lines on only with yes, then one run for lines that never posted. | none |
| R47 | Before a new file message to a first-degree person, skip an open campaign enrollment: pending, active, waiting on a connection, waiting on a signal, or another sender. Search, then fetch the schema. Do not invent an id. If the full list is cut short, read the active slice once. If that is still cut short, refuse. Missing channel data refuses. Page each open status in steps of 25. A short page is not the full list. Sixty pages is the ceiling. An empty page refuses. Do not recheck them the same day. Do not raise a preview credit ceiling to hide a truncated page. | opening: Before a new file message to a first-degree person, see if an active send on this network already has them. Open means pending, active, waiting on a connection, waiting on a signal, or another sender owns them. Tool missing: search, then tools_get. Do not invent an id. Full list cut short: read the active slice once. Still cut short: refuse. Missing channel data refuses the send. Page each open status by 25. A short page is not the full list. Sixty pages is the ceiling. An empty page refuses the send. Do not recheck the same day. Do not raise the preview credit ceiling to hide a truncated page. | tools_get |
| R48 | A request in a direct message or on a connection note never reaches the comment rule. Find those threads since the post went live. Keep a keyword match whose outbound text does not already hold the destination. A keyword hit is not always a request. Drop a thread that only talks about the subject, and one already answered. Draft with the reply skill. Show the queue. Send only texts the owner accepted, oldest first, a few minutes apart. Read the thread again for the URL before each send. | opening: A DM or a connection note never hits the comment rule. Find threads with unibox.inbox.unified_list since the post went live. Keep a keyword match whose outbound text does not already hold the destination URL. A keyword hit is not always a request. Drop subject-only threads and threads already answered. Draft with thread-reply. Show the queue. Send only accepted texts, oldest first, a few minutes apart. Before each send, reread the thread for the URL. | unibox.inbox.unified_list |
| R49 | Private messages are the default. If you will not answer the comments one by one, or comment reads are rate-limited, ask whether the public reply should carry the link. That needs a yes on that post. Do not switch by yourself. Then one reply per request, any degree, oldest first, wording that changes by person. If the owner already replied by hand, follow that wording. About ninety seconds plus jitter, never under the account's gap. Read the daily comment cap before reading comments. If a timed-out reply is under two hours old, stop before any network call. If the network rate-limits you, stop. | opening: Private messages are the default. If you will not answer one by one, hundreds in a day, or comment reads are rate-limited, say so and ask whether that post's public reply should carry the link. That needs yes on that post. Do not change it yourself. Then one public reply per request, any degree, oldest first, wording that changes by person. Follow a hand-written owner reply. About ninety seconds plus jitter, never under the account's own gap. Read the cap before comments so an over-cap pass spends no read. A timed-out reply under two hours old: stop before any network call. Network rate limit: stop. | none |
| R50 | After a live pass, verify with the send list. A public line counts only once it is visible. After a rule change, wait until one later pass finishes. A preview does not prove the repeating path. If the asking comment is gone after the file was verified, there is no public line to post. Mark them done. | opening: After a live pass, verify with social.outreach.keyword_dm_sends.list (artifact_id). A public line counts only once visible on the post. After a rule change, wait until one later pass finishes. A preview alone does not prove the repeating path. If the asking comment is gone after the file was verified, there is no public line. Mark that person done. | social.outreach.keyword_dm_sends.list |
| R51 | An uncertain public write on a busy post ends the pass on purpose. Keep public lines behind the file messages. Reconcile by reading each thread. Reset a row that never posted, and clear its saved intent. | opening: An uncertain public write on a busy post ends the pass on purpose. Keep public lines behind file messages. Reconcile by reading each thread. Reset a row that never posted and clear its saved intent. | none |
| R52 | A scheduled pass fails if it calls a date constructor or the clock on the tick. Read the time text. The local checker does that without those calls. It does not send. | opening: A scheduled pass fails when the tick calls a date constructor or the clock. Read the ISO text. The local checker reads that text without those calls and does not send. | local |
| R53 | A large enrollment read that comes back cut short makes the campaign check refuse. | opening: A large enrollment read cut short forces the campaign check to refuse. | none |
| R54 | If quota enforcement is unavailable, stop. Do not build a side path that performs the same writes. Tell the owner. | opening: If quota enforcement is unavailable, stop. Do not build a side path that does the same writes. Tell the owner. | none |
| R55 | When a run fails, read the run and its events. Take the error text from the last event. Do not invent a field the fetched schema does not have. | opening: On failure, read runs_get and runs_events. Take the error from the last event. Do not invent a field the schema lacks. | runs_get |
| R56 | Also store display name, a first-name column, profile address, comment body, degree, state, enrollment id, reply text, last check, and a note. | gap: columns stay the ones the sheet already has | gap: no fixed import column list |

| G05 | Enable that slug with approval and a credit cap of 25. | gap: this skill does not enable a hosted recipe | gap: the arm is keyword_dm_rules.replace |
| G06 | Audit by calling the workflow, then tail that run. | gap: a preview reads live comments and stores nothing | gap: runs_get and runs_events |
| G07 | Read the ledger with that table-query command. | gap: the record is a worksheet column, not that query | gap: tables.columns.add |
| G08 | Read the failure from a tail field named payload.error. | gap: that field is not on this desk | gap: the last runs_events text |
| G09 | The fallback runner: profile selection, binary override, a lock file, skip when locked, an import shim, stand-down after a hosted scheduled run completes, a per-tool command map, an approved flag, a connection id, and a 64MB buffer. | gap: that runner is not ported | gap: a side path that performs the same writes is refused |
| G10 | Query stored messages on this network with that client filter. | gap: private threads are unibox.inbox.unified_list | gap: unibox.inbox.unified_list |
| G11 | Read identity and the sender list through that client. | gap: the checks stay on whoami and the account list | gap: whoami |

| G13 | A repeating schedule at minutes 7, 22, 37, and 52, with catch-up folded into one. | gap: that schedule and catch-up mode are not restated | gap: this desk does not set that clock |
| G14 | A hosted recipe id, a display name, and a disabled status until enable. | gap: there is no hosted recipe record on this desk | gap: Dreamstate can't do this |

| G16 | Cadence contacts declared on the recipe and mapped in the runner, and never called. | gap: that contact list is not declared | gap: do not activate a sequence in the same step |
